Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the spectral structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 741. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 105 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 741 + O(1/n^105). Summing over all modes and applying the spectral convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 213.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Let ρ be a spectral property functional on the spectral space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 533 and the convergence rate is O(222/n^2).
